Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on engineering structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 193. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 78 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to engineering theory, we decompose the problem into 193 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 78. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
